Found `kirim_orders`/`kirim_lines`/`chiqim_lines`/`chiqim_line_pallets` had **zero UPDATE policy at all** — editing was impossible, not just unbuilt. `chiqim_requests` already had one (`menejer_voids`, 0033): `my_role()='menejer' AND ombor_finished_at IS NULL`, row-level, currently exercised only by the void handler. `kirim_orders.status` flips to `qabul_qilindi` only via a trigger on `gate_weighings.completed_at` (gate stage 2, `complete_kirim_stage2()`, 0013) — stage 1 alone leaves status at `kutilmoqda` even though dated/plated gate photos already exist by then.

**Field buckets, reported and confirmed with the user before writing any SQL:**
- Freely editable: `order_date`/`plate`/`driver` (KIRIM), `request_date`/`plate`/`driver` (CHIQIM) — nothing downstream reads these for a number.
- **Locked, user's explicit call:** `owner_id` on both. Feeds client report/stock-on-hand/yield grouping everywhere on KIRIM; on CHIQIM it additionally scopes the pallet picker — changing it after pallets are reserved would leave reservations pointing at the wrong client's stock. Not included in "freely editable" despite reading as a client-facing field.
- **Locked, not built this pass (flagged, not assumed):** `kirim_lines.declared_qty` (feeds `effective_qty` pre-intake and the Kam chiqdi check), `target_moisture_pct`/`target_so2_mg_kg` (feed lab-overdue exceptions and client-report target-vs-actual), `type_id`; `chiqim_lines.type_id/calibre_id/qty_kg`; `chiqim_line_pallets` reservations. None of these have any UPDATE policy — staying unwritable is what keeps them locked, no new grant touches them.
- Never editable: `serial`/`id` (PK), `status`, `declared_total`, `ombor_finished_at/by`, `voided_at/by`, `created_by/at`.

**Lock boundary, user's explicit call on KIRIM specifically.** CHIQIM reuses the existing void boundary exactly (`ombor_finished_at IS NULL`) — same UI condition, same RLS predicate, per the task's own "same logic as the whitelist" instruction. KIRIM had no precedent to reuse (confirmed: "kirim_orders/gate_weighings have no void mechanism regardless, same standing gap," 2026-07-28 entry). Two options presented: lock once `status` flips to `qabul_qilindi` (matches the literal enum, permits editing through all of stage 1), or lock the moment ANY `gate_weighings` row exists for the order (stricter — the instant Qorovul touches the order at the gate at all). **User chose the stricter option** — dated/plated gate photos already exist the moment stage 1 happens, and letting plate/date drift after that creates a paper-trail mismatch even though it corrupts no number.

**RLS applied** (`supabase/migrations/0038_kirim_chiqim_second_window_edits.sql`):
```sql
create policy menejer_edits on kirim_orders for update to public
  using (my_role() = 'menejer' and not exists (
    select 1 from gate_weighings g where g.order_id = kirim_orders.order_id and g.dir = 'kirim'))
  with check (my_role() = 'menejer' and not exists (
    select 1 from gate_weighings g where g.order_id = kirim_orders.order_id and g.dir = 'kirim'));

drop policy menejer_voids on chiqim_requests;
create policy menejer_updates on chiqim_requests for update to public
  using (my_role() = 'menejer' and ombor_finished_at is null)
  with check (my_role() = 'menejer' and ombor_finished_at is null);
```
`menejer_voids` renamed rather than left alongside a second, functionally-identical UPDATE policy — voiding was never its only real purpose, `handleVoid` and the new `saveEdit` both now go through `menejer_updates`. Both policies are **row-level, not column-restricted** — same acknowledged limitation as the existing `owners`/master-data grants (2026-07-21 entry): the UI is what limits the update payload to date/plate/driver, not the database. Documented explicitly rather than left unstated, matching this codebase's own established practice for every prior row-level grant.

**Audit trail — `audit_log` had existed since Phase 0, unused by any code path until now.** Every successful edit writes one row (`table_name`, `row_id`, `actor`, `action:'edit'`, `before`/`after` — only the fields that actually changed, not a full snapshot) using the table's existing schema and RLS (any signed-in INSERT, rahbar-only SELECT) — no new grant needed. Also auto-posts a human-readable note via the existing append-only `notes`/Qaydlar mechanism (`EntityNotes`'s backing table, already reused elsewhere) so the correction is visible to anyone looking at the order/request in-app, not just queryable by Rahbar. No history-viewer UI was built — that's queryable infrastructure now, a viewer screen is a natural follow-up if wanted, out of scope here.

**UI:** inline edit, same pattern `ClientsSection.tsx` already established (click Tahrirlash → fields become inputs in place → Saqlash/Bekor qilish), placed at the bottom of each row's existing expand panel. The Tahrirlash control simply doesn't render once the lock condition is met — mirrors `FinishedChiqimList.tsx`'s own pre-existing void-button visibility pattern exactly, not a new UI idiom.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` and `npm run lint` clean (same one pre-existing unrelated `AuthProvider.tsx` warning), unit suite 78/78 unchanged. Live-browser-verified end to end, both directions: created a fresh KIRIM order, corrected its date from the second window, confirmed the DB row/`audit_log`/`notes` all updated correctly, confirmed the `Miqdori`/`effective_qty` display was untouched by the edit. Inserted a `gate_weighings` row for that order directly, reloaded, confirmed Tahrirlash no longer renders — **and separately confirmed the RLS itself refuses a direct `.update()` call from the browser's own `window.supabase` client** (`data: []`, no error — the same "blocked write returns zero rows" signature `chiqim-undo-scan.spec.ts` already established), not just a UI-level absence. Repeated the identical sequence for a CHIQIM request (correct date → verify → set `ombor_finished_at` → confirm both Tahrirlash and Bekor qilish disappear together → confirm RLS refusal directly). One incidental finding, not a bug: `useFinishedChiqimRequests.ts` filters out any request whose `plate` starts with `TEST-` by design (2026-07-25 "TEST- prefix hygiene") — a test request created with a `TEST-`-prefixed plate correctly never appeared in the CHIQIM second window at all; renamed the plate to verify the edit UI, unrelated to this task's own change. All test fixtures (KIRIM order, CHIQIM request, the inserted `gate_weighings` row, and their `audit_log`/`notes` rows) deleted afterward via direct SQL — neither table has an in-app delete path. Full Playwright suite run once: 4/5 passed; `full-chain.spec.ts` failed at the same pre-existing Moyka-output race documented in the 2026-07-29 entry, confirmed unrelated via `git status` (this task's diff touches only `KirimOrdersList.tsx`, `FinishedChiqimList.tsx`, and the new migration — zero overlap with the Moyka/lab pipeline).

**Out of scope, not touched (per the task):** the cutting line, opening stock, deleting/voiding requests (pre-existing), editing anything past its lock boundary, a history-viewer UI for `audit_log`.

## 2026-07-30 — Reports showed the wrong date

**Context:** Every "Sana" column across Ombor qoldig'i, Menejer's Hisobot, and Rahbar's Hisobotlar was showing today's date for every row, regardless of when the KIRIM order was actually entered — while the serial passport for the same serial correctly showed the real entered date (e.g. 16.07). Diagnosed by the user as a wrong-field bug, not a data problem.

**Confirmed which field the passport reads before touching anything.** `get_serial_passport`'s headline line is `ko.order_date`/`cr.request_date` — the date the Menejer typed on the KIRIM/CHIQIM form (`SerialPassportModal.tsx` lines 120 and 283). Then read every report view/function live (not the migration history, which doesn't reflect several since-superseded columns) and cross-checked against real data — reproduced the exact symptom: a serial with `kirim_orders.order_date = 2026-07-16` was showing `2026-07-29` (today) in both `report_kirim_rows.date_basis` and `stock_on_hand_rows.anchor_date`.

**Root cause: not literally `created_at`, but the same class of bug — a system-set operational timestamp standing in for the typed date.** `report_kirim_rows.date_basis` was `COALESCE(gate_weighings.stage1_completed_at, kirim_orders.order_date)`, preferring the gate weighing timestamp; `report_chiqim_rows.date_basis` was `gate_weighings.completed_at` with **no entered-date fallback at all**; `stock_on_hand_rows`' raw-not-washed bucket used `storage_intake.confirmed_at`, which defaults to `now()` at accept time — functionally an insertion timestamp despite not being named one. This was SPEC.md §3.2.3's own previously-documented, deliberate design ("arrival date — gate stage 1 completion... falling back to order's own stated date only for the case where stage 1 hasn't happened yet," reasoned to be "in practice unreached, since intake requires stage 1 first") — that assumption doesn't hold whenever order entry and the physical gate event land on different days, which is not a testing artifact, it's the normal case whenever a KIRIM order is created before the truck is actually weighed.

**Checked the whole set before fixing anything, per the task's own instruction — confirmed almost everything shares the same root:**
- `get_client_report`'s raw-side totals (`arrivalDate`, opening/received/processed kg, quality record) reuse `report_kirim_rows.date_basis` directly — inherits the fix automatically, no separate change needed.
- Rahbar dashboard (`rahbar_monthly_trends`/`rahbar_client_ranking`/`rahbar_product_mix`) all aggregate purely off `report_kirim_rows.date_basis`/`report_chiqim_rows.date_basis` — inherits the fix the same way.
- `stock_on_hand_rows`' **finished-pallet** bucket (`finished_pallets.received_date`, set to `new Date()` at pack time in `OmborTayyorTab.tsx:160`) is a genuinely different, correct concept — "how long has this pallet existed in its current packed form," not "when did the raw material arrive." Not typed anywhere, not comparable to `order_date`; changing it would misrepresent real shelf-time. Left alone.
- `yield_rows.completed_date` (Hosildorlik) is already documented in the 2026-07-21 "Yield view" entry / SPEC v1.20 as deliberately the serial's *last cycle completion* date, not arrival — a different, intentional date dimension. Left alone.

**One genuine fork, surfaced and confirmed with the user rather than decided silently:** `get_client_report`'s dispatch-side period bucketing (`client_pallets.departure_date`, `period_dispatch_ids` — which decides which period's opening/produced/dispatched/closing totals a shipment's kg counts toward) also used the gate stage-2 timestamp. Unlike the other fixes, this one wasn't purely a display bug — the frontend (`ClientReportTab.tsx`) already showed `requestDate` primarily, gate completion only secondarily and grayed out — so the *visible* label was already correct; only the *period a shipment's kg gets counted in* was still gate-based. Presented as an explicit choice: leave gate-based (period bucketing reflects real financial movement) vs. switch to `request_date` (keeps the whole document on one consistent basis, since the raw side flips to order-date automatically). **User chose to switch it too.** The "must have actually gate-departed" gate (`cgw.completed_at is not null`) was preserved explicitly in the rewritten SQL, since it had been implicit in the old date comparison and would otherwise have been silently lost by switching to a NOT NULL column.

**Fix applied, surgical — only date columns changed, weight-authority logic (§2.16) untouched:**
```sql
-- report_kirim_rows: no COALESCE needed, order_date is never null
ko.order_date AS date_basis, 'order_date'::text AS date_basis_source

-- report_chiqim_rows
cr.request_date AS date_basis

-- stock_on_hand_rows raw_not_washed bucket — reuses the now-corrected report_kirim_rows
r.date_basis AS anchor_date  -- was (si.confirmed_at)::date

-- get_client_report: client_pallets.departure_date and period_dispatch_ids
-- switched from gate_weighings.completed_at to chiqim_requests.request_date,
-- with cgw.completed_at is not null added explicitly to preserve the
-- "actually departed" gate
```
`supabase/migrations/0039_report_date_basis_use_entered_date.sql`.

**Verification.** Re-ran the live comparison query after applying: all previously-wrong rows now show the entered date exactly (`2026-07-16` stays `2026-07-16` in both `report_kirim_rows.date_basis` and `stock_on_hand_rows.anchor_date`; `date_basis_source` reads `'order_date'` throughout). Smoke-tested `get_client_report` and `report_chiqim_rows` directly via SQL — both run cleanly, no syntax errors from the rewrite. `npx tsc -b --noEmit` and `npm run lint` clean (same one pre-existing `AuthProvider.tsx` warning, unrelated — this task touched no `.ts`/`.tsx` files at all, database-only). Unit suite 78/78 unchanged, including `reportQuery.test.ts`'s `date_basis_source: 'gate_stage1'` case — that test asserts the row-mapper preserves whatever value a DB row carries, not that `'gate_stage1'` is still a value the view can produce, so it stays valid unchanged. Live-browser-verified: a serial entered with a back-dated arrival now shows the same date in its passport, Ombor qoldig'i, Menejer's Hisobot, and Rahbar's Hisobotlar. Full Playwright suite run once.

**Out of scope, not touched (per the task):** the cutting line, opening stock, edit-forms (this task's own scope was date field *sourcing* only, not how dates get entered), `stock_on_hand_rows`' finished-pallet bucket and `yield_rows.completed_date` (both confirmed to be different, correct concepts, not this bug).

## 2026-07-30 — Serial passport: nakladnoy + headline/table restructure

**Context:** The passport (§3.2.5) was missing the KIRIM nakladnoy document, and every section rendered as run-on text paragraphs with large inline photos instead of a scannable structure.

**Part 1 determination, checked before writing anything.** `KirimForm.tsx`'s "Hujjat rasmi · ixtiyoriy — Mijoz nakladnoyasini biriktirish" field has captured the nakladnoy since the KIRIM form was built (`kirim_orders.doc_photo`, `kirim-photos` bucket). Checked Ombor's own `IntakeAcceptForm.tsx` too, per the task's explicit instruction to check both capture points — no document field there, only `pile_photo` (a photo of the physical raw pile, already shown, a different thing). Read `get_serial_passport` directly: its `order` object never selected `doc_photo` at all. **Determination: captured but hidden, not case 2** — no KIRIM-form change needed, confirmed rather than assumed. Fixed with one additive key in the RPC.

**One-section proposal before building everywhere, per the task's own gate.** Built Buyurtma (including the new nakladnoy row) and Darvoza first, verified live end to end — thumbnail loads the real signed photo, click opens a full-resolution lightbox stacked above the passport (z-60 vs the passport's own z-50), Escape closes the lightbox first and the passport on a second press, missing photos produce no row at all. Presented this as the proposed pattern before touching the remaining four sections; **user confirmed apply-to-the-rest as-is.**

**Design decisions, applied once, consistently:**
- **Table shape:** `label | value` rows (`FieldTable`), matching this codebase's own `th`/`td`-table convention (Hisobot, Ombor qoldig'i) rather than inventing a new one. A field/photo that doesn't apply yet is an omitted row, not an empty one — same "missing shows nothing" rule the photos already followed, now extended to every field.
- **Thumbnails:** `GatePhoto.tsx` gained a `thumbnail` + `onOpen` mode — 56×56px, no internal caption (the table row's own label already carries that context, so the old thumbnail's what/who/when caption stack would have been redundant), clicking calls `onOpen(url, label)` instead of the previous `target="_blank"`. Additive and backward-compatible (`onOpen` optional) — confirmed via grep that the passport was the only consumer of `thumbnail={true}` anywhere in the codebase, so nothing else could regress.
- **Lightbox:** new, lives at the outer `SerialPassportModal` level (not inside `PassportBody`'s own scroll container, so it isn't clipped), `z-[60]` stacked above the passport's `z-50`. Backdrop/× both `stopPropagation` before closing, so a lightbox click never also closes the passport underneath it (they're siblings inside the same click-to-close outer div). Escape handling centralized in one listener at the outer level — closes the lightbox first if one is open, the passport otherwise — rather than a second competing listener inside the lightbox itself.
- **Already-tabular content left alone:** the per-pallet breakdown tables (Moyka cycle, Jo'natishlar) and the current-position table were already proper multi-column tables, not run-on text — out of this task's own restructure scope, untouched.
- **The aggregate "Rasmlar" gallery removed, not kept alongside the new inline thumbnails.** It used to show every photo a second time at the bottom; once each section carries its own photos inline, keeping it would mean every photo renders twice. One photo previously shown *only* in that gallery — the CHIQIM lab sample photo (`cycle.lab.samplePhoto`) — was never in the inline Moyka block before this task; folded into the new Moyka lab table so removing the gallery doesn't lose it. `cycle.lab.note` was deliberately **not** added as a new row — it was never shown anywhere before (not even in the old gallery, which is photos-only), and adding previously-unshown text is outside this task's scope (photos + table restructuring only).

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` and `npm run lint` clean (same one pre-existing unrelated `AuthProvider.tsx` warning). Unit suite 78/78 unchanged. Live-browser-verified twice: once on a real KIRIM order with a real nakladnoy (confirmed the thumbnail loads the actual signed photo and the lightbox shows it at native resolution, 1280×960), and once against a full synthetic chain built via direct SQL (moyka_sends → wash_cycles → finished_pallets → lab_results(chiqim) → chiqim_requests → dispatch_manifest → gate_weighings(chiqim), since no live non-test data currently has Moyka/dispatch history) to confirm the Moyka and Jo'natishlar sections — the two not exercised by the nakladnoy test — render correctly with real relational data and no console errors; all synthetic rows deleted afterward via direct SQL. Full Playwright suite run once: 4/5 passed; `full-chain.spec.ts` failed at the same pre-existing Moyka-output race documented in the 2026-07-29 and 2026-07-30 entries, confirmed unrelated via `git status` (this task's diff touches only `SerialPassportModal.tsx`, `GatePhoto.tsx`, `serialPassport.ts`, and the new migration).

**Out of scope, not touched (per the task):** the cutting line, opening stock, edit-forms, report date fields (handled by the immediately prior task) — passport presentation and the nakladnoy document only.

## 2026-07-30 — Netto/Tara terminology

**Context:** Box mass (tara) was already captured per-serial at intake (`storage_intake.box_mass_kg`, since v1.10's §2.16 amendment) and already used internally in the net-weight calc, but was never displayed on its own anywhere — report tables and the serial passport both only ever showed a single net-ish figure, with no visibility into what was subtracted to get there.

**Part 1 determination, checked before writing anything.** `storage_intake` is already joined in `report_kirim_rows` (as `si`) for the existing order-level `total_box_mass_kg` truck-variance CTE — but that's a different, order-level aggregate (summed across every line of a multi-line truck), not this serial's own tara. `si.box_mass_kg` was sitting right there, unselected. `get_serial_passport`'s `intake_row` already does `select * from storage_intake`, so `ir.box_mass_kg` was likewise already available, just not surfaced in the `intake` jsonb object. Confirmed both before writing the migration — purely additive, no new joins needed anywhere.

**A real mislabelling found and fixed before writing any passport code, not assumed from the task text.** The task's literal wording ("rename net/aniq labels to Netto") would have meant renaming the passport's existing "Net" row (`gate_weighings.net_kg`) straight to "Netto." Checked the schema first: `net_kg` is a **generated column**, `gruzheny_kg - pustoy_kg` (loaded minus empty) — it never subtracts box mass. Calling that "Netto" once a real Netto (net of tara) row existed one line below it would have been actively misleading, not just imprecise. Raised this via `AskUserQuestion` rather than guessing or silently deviating from the literal instruction. **User's ruling, verbatim:** *"if it's loaded - emptry, add it as 'Brutto'. if it's loaded- emptry - box mass, then it's netto"* — adopted exactly as the terminology for both the passport and (for consistency) one piece of pre-existing prose in the Buyurtma section that used the word "netto" to describe the same raw gate figure (`"ko'p turdagi reys, darvoza netto hech qachon qabul qilinmaydi"` → corrected to `"darvoza brutto"`).

**Design decisions:**
- **Report tables (Hisobot + Ombor qoldig'i, both Menejer and Rahbar):** "Miqdor, kg" → **NETTO** is a header-only rename (the underlying value, `effective_qty`, was already net — §2.16 — nothing about the number changes). New **TARA** column reads `report_rows.box_mass_kg` (added to `report_kirim_rows`; hardcoded `null` in `report_chiqim_rows`, since a finished pallet's own weight has already had its serial's box mass deducted upstream at the raw stage — it has no tara of its own to show). `—` for any row with no box mass on file (not yet accepted by Ombor, or CHIQIM). Same treatment applied to `stock_on_hand_rows` and the Excel export (`reportExport.ts`) for consistency with the on-screen table, since it exports the same rows — a judgment call, not explicitly asked, flagged as such when reporting back.
- **Totals strip:** user explicitly confirmed adding a **Tara** total (*"Yes, add a Tara total"*) alongside the existing Kirim/Chiqim/Neto figures. `report_totals`'s return shape gained a 4th column (`total_kg_tara`), which required an explicit `DROP FUNCTION` before recreating it — Postgres refuses `CREATE OR REPLACE` when a function's `RETURNS TABLE` shape changes (`42P13`). Confirmed the whole multi-statement migration rolled back atomically on the first failed attempt (re-queried `information_schema.columns` and found zero partial changes) before reapplying with the fix.
- **Serial passport — KIRIM Darvoza section only** gains the three-row breakdown (Brutto → Tara → Netto, in that reading order, loaded/empty rows first) — deliberately **not** added to the dispatch gate blocks in Jo'natishlar, since a dispatched pallet's weight has no box mass left to subtract (already gone at the raw stage) and adding an always-empty Tara/Netto pair there would just be noise. `buildGateRows` took an optional 5th `taraKg` param for this reason: omitted entirely (`undefined`) at the Jo'natishlar call site → Brutto rename only; passed (`intake?.boxMassKg ?? null`) at the Darvoza call site → full three-row breakdown, with `—`/"kutilmoqda" fallbacks for a serial whose gate data exists but whose intake hasn't happened yet (gate happens before intake in the KIRIM flow, so this is a real, not hypothetical, ordering).
- **Qabul qilish:** "Aniq (Ombor)" → **Netto (Ombor)** (confirmed correct, not just assumed, by reading `IntakeAcceptForm.tsx`'s own pre-existing code comment: *"§2.16 box mass (KIRIM only): net product = darvoza netto − quti massasi"* — `actual_qty` already represented a net-of-product reading conceptually). New **Tara** row added directly below it, same `box_mass_kg` value the Darvoza section's Tara row shows (same serial, same source column — deliberately the same number in both places, not two independent reads).
- **Deliberately out of scope:** `OmborHisobotlar.tsx`/`IntakeDetailView.tsx` (Ombor's own separate operational history/detail screens, not named in the task — only "Ombor qoldig'i", "Menejer/Rahbar reports", and "serial passport" were), and `ReportRowCard.tsx` (the phone-width card view of the same Hisobot data) — it has no literal "Miqdor" header text to rename (the qty renders inline in a compact one-line summary), and the task's own testing checklist didn't call out mobile cards. Flagged, not silently dropped.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` and `npm run lint` clean (same one pre-existing unrelated `AuthProvider.tsx` warning). Unit suite 80/80 (78 pre-existing + 2 new: `boxMassKg` mapping for KIRIM rows with and without a box-mass value, and the CHIQIM-always-null case) — also had to backfill `box_mass_kg: null` into the two `ReportDbRow` test fixture builders in `reportQuery.test.ts` once the field became non-optional on the type. Live-browser-verified against real data (serial `280726-029`, `box_mass_kg=412.6`): report row shows NETTO 6,427.4 kg / TARA 412.6 kg; passport shows Yuk bilan 11,700 / Bo'sh 4,860 / **Brutto 6,840** / **Tara 412.6** / **Netto 6,427.4** — arithmetic checks out exactly (6,840 − 412.6 = 6,427.4) and matches the report row's own NETTO value precisely; Qabul qilish shows matching Netto (Ombor) 6,434 kg / Tara 412.6 kg. Also checked the pending-intake case (serial `290726-069`, gate done but Ombor hasn't accepted yet): report row TARA shows `—`, totals-strip Tara total correctly excludes it, passport Darvoza shows Brutto 8,660 / Tara `—` / Netto "kutilmoqda", Qabul qilish shows "Hali qabul qilinmagan." Checked both Rahbar (`/rahbar/hisobotlar`) and Menejer (`/menejer/hisobot`) — identical shared component, identical output. Full Playwright suite run once (see test run log for this session).

## 2026-07-31 — Raw dispatch (feature 1 of 4, agreed order: raw dispatch → opening-stock seed → Rezka → KN origin)

**Context.** A client can collect raw (unwashed) material directly on a CHIQIM request — e.g. half a load washed, half taken raw. Washing-path raw only; raw KN destined for cutting (Rezka) is explicitly out of scope, per the earlier inspect-and-plan report on Rezka/raw-dispatch/KN-origin. Built against the confirmed design from that report's own point 3, with two schema/SQL checkpoints reviewed and confirmed by the user before applying (raw-balance approach + `calibre_id` handling; then `report_rows`/`get_client_report`), per this task's own "show all SQL and wait for confirmation" instruction.

**A — Generalized raw-balance arithmetic, built once.** New sibling table `raw_dispatch_lines` (id, chiqim_line_id, serial, weight_kg, box_mass_kg, `net_kg` generated as weight−box_mass, loaded_at, created_by) — shaped like `moyka_sends`, not a generic `raw_exits` table with a destination enum: the two exits share almost no columns (raw dispatch needs box mass + a line link `moyka_sends` has no use for), and this codebase already keeps `dispatch_manifest`/`chiqim_line_pallets` as separate purpose-shaped tables rather than one polymorphic reservation table. `useMoykaSerials.ts` — the ONE hook every consumer already reads — gained a third fetch and `available = max(0, inputKg − sent − rawDispatched)`; both the Moyka send screen and the new CHIQIM raw picker (`ChiqimForm.tsx`) read this same hook, so they can never compute two different numbers for the same serial's balance. Two view-level fixes found while tracing every existing "Σ moyka_sends" consumer, not in the literal ask, flagged before touching: `stock_on_hand_rows`' `raw_not_washed` bucket and `wip_rows`' `raw_not_sent` CTE both needed the identical `− raw_dispatch_lines` term, or they'd silently overstate raw stock/WIP once any of it had been raw-dispatched instead of sent to Moyka. `yield_rows.raw_consumed_kg` deliberately left at `Σ moyka_sends` only — yield measures process loss for material that actually went through Moyka; raw collected directly bypasses washing and correctly never enters that calculation.

**B — `calibre_id` handling.** `chiqim_lines.calibre_id` made nullable; new `raw_serial text references kirim_lines(serial)`; check constraint enforcing exactly one of the two is set (`chiqim_lines_calibre_xor_raw`) — not a separate line-kind column, since `raw_serial is not null` already unambiguously means "raw line" and doubles as the pointer to its source serial, mirroring how a finished line's own identity lives in what it points to (`chiqim_line_pallets`), not a redundant tag. `qty_kg` stays required for both kinds — for a raw line it's Menejer's planned figure, never overwritten once Ombor loads; the actual collected amount lives in `raw_dispatch_lines`, read separately. Ripple: 8 files actually touched `chiqim_lines.calibre_id` or its derived TS types (`ChiqimForm.tsx`, `OmborChiqimTab.tsx`, `FinishedChiqimList.tsx`, `useOmborChiqimRequests.ts`, `useFinishedChiqimRequests.ts`, `useChiqimTrips.ts`, `chiqimScan.ts`, `chiqimScan.test.ts`) — every other calibre_id/calibreId match in the initial 38-file grep was `finished_pallets.calibre_id` (always non-null, no ripple).

**C — Ombor's raw entry, and the confirmed decision on when it's written.** Weight + box-mass inputs, no scan zone, no `dispatch_manifest` row — a genuinely distinct render branch inside `OmborChiqimTab.tsx`'s existing request card, not a forced-generic line renderer. **Confirmed with the user before building:** entries are held client-side (`rawDraftByRequest`, same shape as the existing `scannedByRequest` scan list — editable/removable via a Tozalash ✕ button) and only batch-inserted into `raw_dispatch_lines` at `Yuklashni yakunlash`, the same commit point `dispatch_manifest` already uses. This is *why* `raw_dispatch_lines` is safely append-only with no update/delete policy — a typo is corrected by clearing the draft pre-commit, not by editing a written row; an immediate-write design would have made a typo permanently corrupt that serial's balance. Ombor's entered net is **authoritative immediately** — a second explicit decision, confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` over the alternative (gate-net-derived, provisional-then-final, cloning `weightAuthority.ts`'s KIRIM shape) — because CHIQIM's real gate weighing already treats gate net as a reconciliation check on the manifest total for pallets, never a value source (SPEC §5.4); making raw symmetric with that existing rule avoided a second weight-authority-shaped system for a benefit (gate-verified raw figures) the original task text only mentioned once, in passing.

**Over-collection warning (2026-07-31 feedback).** Nothing stopped Ombor entering more raw than a serial had left — `max(0, ...)` floors the balance and the excess would vanish with no record. Fixed with a non-blocking warning (same `useMoykaSerials` hook, so it can never disagree with the picker/balance shown elsewhere): a persistent "Bu seriyada qolgan: X kg" caption plus a `StatusNote` when the entered net exceeds it. Never blocks, matching this app's warn-never-block convention everywhere else (Kam chiqdi, Tugallash warnings, CHIQIM shortfall).

**D — Lab gate.** Confirmed true by construction, no RLS change needed: raw dispatch never touches `finished_pallets`/`wash_cycles`, and the hard gate (`ombor_writes` INSERT policy on `finished_pallets`) only guards Barcode #2 assignment — raw never reaches that table at all.

**E — Reporting.** New `report_rows` kind `chiqim_raw` (one row per `raw_dispatch_lines` entry, same granularity convention as the other two kinds), unioned via a new `report_raw_dispatch_rows` view; reuses the existing `'jonatilgan'` pallet-status vocabulary rather than inventing a raw-only status, so the Hisobot status filter picks it up for free. `get_client_report` gained a third closing-balance term (`closingKg = opening + received − processed − rawDispatched`, both total and per-type) and a new `raw.dispatches` array — deliberately a **sibling** to the existing (pallet-based) top-level `dispatches`, never merged, so raw-taken and washed-and-collected stay visually distinct per the task's own requirement. Passport gained a parallel `rawDispatches` array, same headline+FieldTable pattern, its own section under Jo'natishlar (omitted entirely when empty, matching every other "missing shows nothing" section on that screen).

**`total_kg_tara` split, not left ambiguous (2026-07-31 feedback).** `report_totals`' single `total_kg_tara` summed KIRIM tara (boxes in) and raw-dispatch tara (boxes out) — unrelated quantities that happen to share a column, correct on a direction-filtered view but meaningless summed together on "both." Split into `total_kg_tara_in`/`total_kg_tara_out`; `TotalsStrip.tsx` now shows both labeled figures instead of one merged "Tara," and the Excel export gained a second tara row.

**🐛 Real bug found and fixed during hand-verification, not by inspection.** `report_filtered_rows`' direction predicate was `p_direction = 'both' or r.kind = p_direction` — selecting the CHIQIM direction specifically (one of only three Hisobot options) requires `r.kind = 'chiqim'` exactly, which `'chiqim_raw'` never satisfies. Confirmed live against seeded fixture data: `report_totals('chiqim', ...)` returned `total_count=0` against rows `report_totals('both', ...)` correctly counted — raw dispatch rows would have silently vanished from Hisobot the instant anyone filtered to CHIQIM specifically, the single most likely filter choice, not an edge case. Fixed (`0045_raw_dispatch_direction_filter_fix.sql`): `r.kind = p_direction or (p_direction = 'chiqim' and r.kind = 'chiqim_raw')`. Re-verified: `report_totals('chiqim', ...)` now returns the correct `total_count=2`/`total_kg_out=550`/`total_kg_tara_out=40` for the same fixture.

**🚩 Flagged for the future, per the user's own instruction.** `raw_dispatch_lines.serial` FKs to `kirim_lines(serial)` — the same constraint the Rezka inspection report identified as needing to move to a serial-identity anchor table once Rezka source-2 (cutting-batch-minted serials with no `kirim_lines` row) lands. Fine today — every serial that can raw-dispatch arrived by a real truck — flagged here so the re-pointing isn't a surprise when Rezka is built.

**Hand-verification against seeded data (before cleanup).** Built a live fixture directly via SQL under the codebase's existing dedicated QA owner ("Sinov Mijozi QA") — a **non**-`TEST-`-prefixed plate (`QA-RAWDISPATCH-0731`) deliberately, since `report_kirim_rows`/`stock_on_hand_rows`/`report_raw_dispatch_rows`/`get_client_report` (via its join to `report_kirim_rows`) all filter `TEST-%` plates, which would have made the fixture invisible to exactly the arithmetic being checked. One KIRIM order, two lines/serials (1000 kg and 600 kg, both non-provisional — gate + box mass both known), a partial Moyka send (400 kg on the first serial only, deliberately matching the task's own "partial Moyka send plus partial raw dispatch on one serial" scenario), and one CHIQIM request mixing two raw lines (one per serial: 300 kg / 250 kg planned, entered as 320−20 and 270−20 kg to also exercise the box-mass subtraction) — directly testing "a truck taking from two serials leaves the right remainder on each."
- `stock_on_hand_rows`: serial 1 → 300 kg (1000−400−300 ✓), serial 2 → 350 kg (600−0−250 ✓) — each serial's own remainder correct.
- `get_client_report`: `receivedKg=1600`, `rawDispatchedKg=550`, `closingKg=1050` — `0+1600−0−550=1050` exactly; the formula itself is correct.
- **Found and reported, not glossed over:** `get_client_report`'s `raw.closingKg` and `stock_on_hand_rows`' raw bucket do **not** agree in this exact scenario (1050 vs. 650 combined) — and this is pre-existing, not something raw dispatch introduced. `get_client_report`'s `processed` term only fires once a wash cycle's `completed_date` lands (i.e., once washing *finishes*), so the 400 kg already sent to Moyka but not yet washed-complete still counts as "raw" in the client report's balance; `stock_on_hand_rows` instead subtracts the moment material is *sent* to Moyka, since from then on it's a different WIP state (`wip_rows.moyka_not_returned`), not "in raw storage." Confirmed pre-existing by checking the identical gap exists with the raw-dispatch terms removed entirely — the two views have always measured different things ("not yet turned into output" vs. "not yet sent anywhere"). Raw dispatch's own new term is symmetric and correct in both models; it neither introduces nor fixes this gap, and fixing it is out of scope for this task.
- `report_raw_dispatch_rows`/passport/`report_totals`: all matched expected values exactly (see the bug entry above for the one real miss, now fixed).
Fixture deleted afterward via direct SQL (not voided) — `kirim_orders`/`kirim_lines`/`storage_intake`/`moyka_sends`/`chiqim_requests`/`chiqim_lines`/`raw_dispatch_lines` all have no void mechanism (a gap this codebase has flagged before, 2026-07-17 entry), and this was one-off verification scaffolding created and deleted within the same turn, not a regression fixture meant to persist — flagged here rather than silently deleted without a trace in this log.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` clean throughout. Unit suite grew from 80 to 82 (two new `mapDbRowToReportRow` cases for the `chiqim_raw` kind) plus `chiqimScan.test.ts`'s existing fixtures updated for the widened `ChiqimLineLike` type — 82/82. Reporting arithmetic hand-verified against live seeded data as above, including one real bug found and fixed. Full Playwright suite run once: 3/5 passed; the two failures (`chiqim-undo-scan.spec.ts`, `full-chain.spec.ts`) both trace to the identical root cause, confirmed independently of this task's diff — the shared fixture owner both tests select (`E2E_OWNER_NAME = 'Boysun Quritilgan Mevalar'`, `tests/e2e/helpers/fixtures.ts`) is currently `active=false` in the live database (confirmed via direct SQL), so `ChiqimForm.tsx`'s Buyurtmachi dropdown (active-only, `useOwners()` with no `includeInactive`) never offers it — same failure mode both times, pre-existing, and no migration in this task touches `owners`. Flagged, not silently worked around or fixed unilaterally: reactivating a client is a real business-state change, not something to flip as a side effect of an unrelated feature.

**Out of scope, not touched (per the task and the agreed feature order):** Rezka, KN origin/state tracking, opening-stock seeding, the serial-identity anchor table.

## 2026-07-31 — SPEC.md re-wash cleanup + owners housekeeping audit

**Context.** SPEC.md still described the pre-2026-07-28 re-wash design (numbered wash cycles, Ombor void-and-resend, `1-yuvish (bekor qilindi) → 2-yuvish` passport rendering) in dozens of places even though that design was removed wholesale by the "Lab moves inside Moyka" migration three days earlier — the migration itself never got a SPEC.md pass or a changelog row. Two-part task: rewrite SPEC.md against the migrations/DECISIONS.md as ground truth (explicitly *not* against SPEC's own prose, which was the thing being fixed), and separately audit `owners` for fake/test client records ahead of a user-confirmed delete.

**Approach — a workflow, not a solo read-through, given the stakes.** Ultracode was on for this task. Ran a two-phase workflow: phase 1 spawned two independent investigator agents in parallel (one full-text SPEC.md audit against `0035`/`0036`/DECISIONS.md, one live-DB owners/FK audit via the Supabase MCP `execute_sql` tool); phase 2 spawned two independent verifiers, each re-deriving the first pass's claims from the same ground truth rather than trusting the findings at face value. Total: 4 agents, ~440K tokens, 53 tool calls, ~43 minutes. This caught two real errors before anything was applied (see below) and confirmed everything else — worth the cost for a spec-accuracy task the user framed as "anyone planning from the current text would build a design that doesn't exist" and a destructive-adjacent database audit.

### Part 1 — SPEC.md

**40+ passages rewritten or removed**, every one wrapped in this doc's own pre-existing `~~strikethrough~~`-for-superseded-text convention (old wording kept visible, never silently deleted, per CLAUDE.md's own rule) — spanning the header status banner, §1.1, §2.13 (the core re-wash-loop section, replaced wholesale), §2.14, §2.16.1, §3.2.2 (×2), §3.2.5 (×2), §3.2.6 (×2, one covering two of the five state bullets), §3.2.7 (×5), §3.2.8 (×4, one covering the dry-matter paragraph found only during my own final read-through, not by either agent pass), §3.2.9 (×2), §3.2.10, §5.2, §5.3 (×4), §5.5's KIRIM/CHIQIM comparison table, §5.5.3 (×3), §5.5.4 (replaced wholesale), §5.5.5 (replaced wholesale), §5.5.6, §6.3, §7 item 8, and §8's `FINISHED_PALLETS`/`WASH_CYCLES`/availability-view entries.

**Two corrections caught by the independent verification pass, not applied as originally proposed:**
1. §3.2.7 Section B (client report quality record) — the first pass claimed the dropped wash-cycle-number field was replaced by a "re-tested Ha/Yo'q badge." False: that badge exists only on the serial passport and yield table (confirmed against `SerialPassportModal.tsx`/`YieldTable.tsx` via DECISIONS.md's own "Misleading, fixed" bullet); `get_client_report`'s `quality_record`/`delivered_lab` CTE (0036) returns only `moisturePct`/`so2MgKg`/`verdict`/`sampleDate` — the field is dropped outright on this one screen, nothing replaces it. Corrected text applied instead.
2. §3.2.10 (Rahbar monthly trends re-wash rate) — the first pass claimed `max_cycle_no` was "dropped entirely from `yield_rows`." False at the SQL level: `0035`'s `yield_rows` view still literally selects `1 as max_cycle_no`, hardcoded for shape compatibility (same pattern as the passport's `cycleNo`) — only the *TS-layer* field was dropped. Corrected text applied; the substantive point (re-wash rate now keys off the boolean `rewashed`, not a cycle count) was already right.

**Two more stale passages found only during verification's own independent re-sweep** (missed by the first investigate pass entirely): a dangling citation at §2.16.1 to `src/lib/rewash.ts`/`activeCycles.ts` as a still-live implementation precedent — both files were deleted outright in the 2026-07-28 migration (confirmed against DECISIONS.md's own "Deleted outright" list); and two of §3.2.6's five state-bullets ("Available," "Awaiting lab") using "current (wash) cycle" phrasing that the first pass's own finding for that same five-bullet list had only actually covered for the other two bullets.

**A real version-number collision found and fixed while touching the changelog.** The previous day's raw-dispatch feature (§3.1/§5.4/§8) had been inline-tagged `v1.26` — colliding with the box-mass/tara feature's own, already-changelogged `v1.26` (2026-07-30). Retagged the four raw-dispatch mentions `v1.27` and gave it its own changelog row (previously missing entirely — raw dispatch had shipped with zero §9 entry). Also added a `v1.28` row for this cleanup itself, explicitly noting it retroactively logs the 2026-07-28 migration (same "retroactively logged" precedent `v1.16`'s own row already used for an identical gap). Header **Version:** bumped `1.21` → `1.28` — it had drifted five releases stale (the field was last touched in the v1.21 build, 2026-07-21, and never updated by any of the six later changelog rows already in the table).

**Deliberately left alone, not silently missed:** §5.5.3's "the batch will sit in re-wash limbo" sentence (a genuine, still-open design note about the lack of a write-off path, not a description of the removed mechanism itself) and §6.4's `handleRewash`-bug paragraph (already explicitly framed by the doc's own text as "kept as historical record of the v1.21 build" — correcting it would defeat the reason it's kept). §3.4/§3.5 (Kuzatuv/old client-report design) were not touched either — both are already marked superseded by §3.2 elsewhere in the doc and read as historical design record, not live spec, matching how staleness there was already being handled before this task. Older changelog rows (`v1.12`–`v1.21`) were left untouched on principle — a changelog entry is a historical record of what shipped at that version, not a place to retroactively "correct" once later work supersedes it.

### Part 2 — owners audit and delete

**FK picture, confirmed by two independent sweeps (`information_schema` join + `pg_constraint`/OID lookup):** exactly two foreign keys anywhere in the public schema reference `owners` — `kirim_orders.owner_id` and `chiqim_requests.owner_id`, both `NO ACTION`. No other table blocks a delete.

**Critical finding beyond the literal ask:** `public.owners` has row-level security enabled with no DELETE policy at all (`menejer_owners_insert`/`menejer_owners_update`/`read_all` only, confirmed live via `pg_policies`/`pg_class.relrowsecurity`). A DELETE issued as a normal authenticated app role affects zero rows regardless of FK state — owner deletion was deliberately removed from the app's RLS surface at some point, deactivate-only by design (per `tests/e2e/helpers/fixtures.ts`'s own comment). Only a service-role/superuser connection (this session's Supabase MCP access) can actually delete a row, and only after any referencing `kirim_orders`/`chiqim_requests` rows are cleared first.

**Seven owners, current state:**
| Owner | Active | Ops rows | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Global Export Company | yes | 5 kirim_orders (real data) | blocked_by_fk — the one live client, excluded from delete consideration by the task's own scope |
| Boysun Quritilgan Mevalar | no | 0 | **deactivate_only** — the live `E2E_OWNER_NAME` fixture; 6 Playwright spec files select it by exact UI label (`full-chain`, `chiqim-undo-scan`, `report-effective-qty-parity`, `lab-packing-hard-gate`, `lab-relocation-loss-verification`, `helpers/fixtures.ts`) to seed real KIRIM/CHIQIM data every run; deleting it breaks the suite immediately |
| Nukus Agro Eksport | no | 0 | **deactivate_only** — FK-safe today, but named as the fixture for a hand-computed client-report balance walkthrough documented at length in this log (search "Nukus"); `fixtures.ts` explains Boysun was picked over Nukus specifically to avoid perturbing it |
| Sinov Mijozi QA | no | 0 | safe_to_delete — already flagged as test debris in this log's own 2026-07-20 entry, deliberately left pending exactly this kind of decision |
| Farg'ona Eksport Guruhi | no | 0 | safe_to_delete — part of the `demo-data-2026-07-20.sql` 5-owner seed set, no unique dependency found |
| Samarqand Meva Kompaniyasi | no | 0 | safe_to_delete — same seed set, no unique dependency |
| Toshkent Agro Savdo | no | 0 | safe_to_delete — same seed set, no unique dependency |

Full per-owner counts, the FK inventory, and the Boysun/Nukus dependency detail were reported to the user directly (not duplicated here) before any write. **User's decisions:** keep Boysun and Nukus deactivated as-is — declared test/reference infrastructure, same treatment as the `.env.test` role accounts, no code change; proceed with deleting the 4 confirmed-safe owners.

**Executed.** Row counts re-verified immediately before the delete (defensive re-check — time had passed since the audit) — still 0/0 kirim_orders/chiqim_requests on all four. `DELETE FROM owners WHERE id IN (...)` run via this session's service-role DB connection (required — confirmed above, no app-role DELETE policy exists on `owners`), all 4 rows removed in one statement: Sinov Mijozi QA, Farg'ona Eksport Guruhi, Samarqand Meva Kompaniyasi, Toshkent Agro Savdo. Final `owners` table: 3 rows — Global Export Company (active, the one real client), Boysun Quritilgan Mevalar (inactive, kept), Nukus Agro Eksport (inactive, kept). No void mechanism exists for `owners` (deactivate-only by the app's own RLS design, per the finding above) — a real DELETE, not a soft-delete, is the only way this table supports removal at all, which is exactly why the pre-delete re-verification and the user's own explicit go-ahead mattered here.

**Verification.** SPEC.md changes are prose-only, no build/test surface to run. Owners audit was read-only throughout its investigate/verify passes (both explicitly instructed not to write); re-verified independently before this entry was written — every owner's row counts reproduced exactly, no discrepancies. Post-delete state confirmed via a fresh `select * from owners`.

## 2026-08-01 — Client report three-bucket raw balance

**Context.** The client report and Ombor qoldig'i subtracted raw at different moments — qoldig'i at Moyka *send*, the client report at wash *completion* — so a serial mid-processing showed different raw figures on the two screens; a client reading their statement mid-processing saw more raw than physically existed. This exact gap had already been found and explicitly deferred as out-of-scope during the raw-dispatch task (2026-07-31 entry above, "Found and reported, not glossed over" — 1050 vs. 650 kg on the same fixture shape). Fix: three buckets instead of two — Xom (omborda), Moykada (in Moyka, not yet packed), Tayyor (finished) — both screens now subtract raw at send, so they agree, and material in transit is visible instead of vanishing.

**Approach — verified before applying, not a quick patch.** Per the task's own instruction (this function has one documented shipped loss-calculation bug, cited in its own migration header, and was rewritten twice already this session for Laborator-v2 and raw dispatch) and ultracode being on, the first SQL draft went through an independent verification workflow (schema review + reconciliation stress test, two parallel agents) *before* being shown to the user. Caught 3 blocking bugs and 4 minor issues in the draft — see below — none of which had reached the database.

**Bugs found and fixed pre-apply:**
1. `raw_closing_total`/`_by_type` and the new `moykada_total`/`_by_type` summed over every `client_lines` row unfiltered by period end — missing the `arrival_date <= p_to` bound `raw_opening_total`/`raw_received_total` already had. Fixed.
2. `stock_on_hand_rows` (qoldig'i) INNER JOINs `storage_intake` — a serial that's arrived but not yet formally accepted into raw storage is invisible in qoldig'i entirely. The draft had no equivalent gate, so a declared-but-not-yet-accepted line would count toward the client report's Xom but never appear in qoldig'i — closing one disagreement while opening a new one. Fixed: `client_lines` gained a `has_intake` flag (`storage_intake` existence), and Xom/Moykada/the new reconciliation object are all gated on it, matching qoldig'i's own join exactly. `receivedKg` deliberately stays *un*-gated — it answers "did the client's goods arrive," not "is it in tracked raw storage," a different question.
3. Exact equality with qoldig'i is architecturally scoped to `p_to = today`: qoldig'i is an unconditional live snapshot with no period concept at all; the client report is period-bounded. For a genuinely historical `p_to` the two answer different questions by design (balance as of that past date vs. balance right now) and will diverge if anything moved since — not a defect, and "fixing" it would mean making qoldig'i period-aware, which was explicitly out of scope. The user's own worked example is inherently a same-day scenario, so this covers the stated requirement.
4. (Minor, ruled out rather than fixed.) A theoretical divergence if a serial could be split across multiple `client_lines` rows — confirmed impossible in this schema: `kirim_lines.serial` is that table's own primary key, so `client_lines` (one row per `kirim_lines` row) always has exactly one row per serial.
5. (Minor, addressed via a new field rather than a UI patch.) An oversent serial (sent to Moyka beyond what it ever received) would floor Xom at 0 while Moykada stays unfloored, a display-level gap a client could notice by subtracting the shown figures themselves. Rather than a new warning banner, the new `reconciliation.balancesKg` field (below) surfaces this automatically — any oversend anomaly makes it go non-zero, extending the existing `capped_serials` mechanism's coverage to before Tugallash fires, for free.
6. (Minor, defensive.) Moykada's finalization gate now checks `wash_cycle_status = 'final' and finalized_at is not null` together, not `finalized_at` alone — confirmed the app's own Tugallash handler (`OmborTayyorTab.tsx:176-190`) already writes both atomically in one upsert, so this is belt-and-suspenders, not a behavior change.
7. (Minor, unchanged deliberately.) The `(finalized_at at time zone 'utc')::date <= p_to` cast carries the same Tashkent/UTC boundary edge case already present elsewhere in this codebase (`report_kirim_rows`' `gw.completed_at`) — left as-is, matching existing convention rather than introducing a one-off fix in only this function.

**New self-check, not just a one-time hand-verify.** Given the bug history, `get_client_report` now returns a `raw.reconciliation` object on every call: `totalReceivedKg`, `xomKg`, `moykadaKg`, `cumulativeOutputKg`, `cumulativeLossKg`, `cumulativeRawDispatchedKg`, and `balancesKg` (their difference — 0 when everything reconciles, non-zero flags a real anomaly). All five terms are cumulative-as-of-`p_to` and `has_intake`-gated identically, so they're apples-to-apples rather than mixing period-scoped and all-time figures. The reconciliation identity needed **five** terms, not three — an early draft check missed that raw dispatch is a distinct third exit path alongside Moyka processing and direct loss, and would show a false imbalance without it.

**Display/export (requirement 5).** `ClientReportRaw`/`ClientReportRawByType` gained `sentToMoykaKg`/`moykadaKg` (plus the new `ClientReportReconciliation` type). `ClientReportTab.tsx`: the raw balance chain now reads opening → +received → −sentToMoyka → −rawDispatched → =closing (Xom), followed by a bold Moykada line; the old processed/loss breakdown moved below as an informational block (still period-scoped by wash-completion date, unchanged), no longer part of the subtraction chain. byType table gained sentToMoyka/closing/moykada columns. Uz/Ru labels added (`sentToMoyka`, `moykada`); `dateBasisRaw` extended with a clause for the closing balance's new send-date basis. Excel export mirrors the UI restructure exactly, byType columns included. Reconciliation is not surfaced in the UI — it's an internal self-check field, not a user-facing requirement.

**Hand-verification against live seeded data.** Built directly via SQL under Nukus Agro Eksport (the owner already named in this log, and reconfirmed above, as the dedicated hand-computed-client-report-balance fixture) — non-`TEST-`-prefixed plates (`QA-3BUCKET-0801-A`/`-B`), since `report_kirim_rows` filters `TEST-%`. Two serials, `p_from = p_to = 2026-08-01` (today, matching the equality scope in point 3): serial A = 10,000 kg, 5,000 sent to Moyka, washing incomplete — the user's own exact worked numbers; serial B = 8,000 kg, 3,000 sent to Moyka *and* 2,000 raw-dispatched, exercising the mixed-exit-path case.
- Before Tugallash: `stock_on_hand_rows` → A: 5,000 kg, B: 3,000 kg. `get_client_report` → Xom A: 5,000, Xom B: 3,000 — **exact match**. Moykada A: 5,000, B: 3,000 (nothing packed yet). `reconciliation`: `totalReceivedKg=18000`, `xomKg=8000`, `moykadaKg=8000`, `cumulativeRawDispatchedKg=2000`, `balancesKg=0`.
- Tugallash fired on serial A: 4,500 kg packed, `final_loss_pct=10`. Re-queried: Xom **unchanged** at 5,000/8,000 (never touched by Tugallash, as designed); Moykada A dropped to 0, combined moykada 8,000→3,000 (exactly B's untouched amount); `processedBreakdown.lossKg=500`/`lossPct=10`/`calibreKg=4500`; `reconciliation.cumulativeOutputKg=4500`, `cumulativeLossKg=500`, `balancesKg=0` — the 5,000 kg cleanly resolved into 4,500 Tayyor + 500 loss, exactly the "loss comes out of the Moykada→Tayyor transition, not out of raw" requirement.
- `stock_on_hand_rows` re-checked post-Tugallash: still 5,000/3,000 raw_not_washed — confirms Tugallash never perturbs Xom on either screen.
- Fixture deleted afterward via direct SQL (same no-void-mechanism precedent as the raw-dispatch fixture) — all seven touched tables re-checked at 0 rows remaining.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` clean. Unit suite: 82/82 (unaffected — no covered logic touched). Full Playwright suite run once: 4/5 passed; the one failure (`full-chain.spec.ts`, Ombor's "Moykada — chiqishi kutilmoqda" awaiting-output row not appearing) is in a code path this task never touched (`OmborTayyorTab.tsx`'s own Moyka-output list, not `get_client_report` or any of the five files this task edited) — flagged as a pre-existing/unrelated failure, not chased further, per the task's own explicit scope.

**Out of scope, not touched:** Rezka, KN origin/state, opening-stock seeding, Ombor UI polish, `stock_on_hand_rows` itself (confirmed already correct — this task made the client report match it, not the reverse).

## 2026-08-02 — Raw dispatch serial pool

**Context.** The one-serial-per-raw-line model (`chiqim_lines.raw_serial`, 2026-07-31) forced Menejer to pin one exact serial with an implied quantity before the truck ever arrived. Reworked to mirror the finished side structurally: `chiqim_line_pallets` is a reservation pool, `dispatch_manifest` is what actually loaded — raw becomes the same shape, a Menejer-selected POOL of candidate serials (`chiqim_line_raw_serials`, new) with Ombor recording actual per-serial draws on the floor (`raw_dispatch_lines`, unchanged). The pool's total available has no relationship to what gets drawn — "these are your sources," not an allocation.

**Schema.** New junction table `chiqim_line_raw_serials(line_id, serial)`, deliberately NOT copying `chiqim_line_pallets`' reservation machinery (`released_at`, active-unique-index) — raw is bulk and divisible, so two requests may legitimately draw from the same serial; there is nothing to reserve or release, only pool membership. `chiqim_lines` gained `line_kind` ('finished'|'raw'), backfilled from the old `calibre_id`/`raw_serial` XOR shape before `raw_serial` was dropped; every existing raw line's single serial migrated into the pool as a one-row pool (by construction, every historical `raw_dispatch_lines` draw already matched it exactly, so nothing became retroactively "out of pool"). `qty_kg` became nullable for raw lines (Menejer may not know how much will be drawn) via one folded check constraint (`chiqim_lines_kind_shape`) replacing the XOR. `0047_raw_dispatch_serial_pool.sql`.

**App-level guard (2026-08-02 feedback).** The DB can't enforce "a raw line has ≥1 pooled serial" across two tables — `ChiqimForm.tsx` gained an explicit pre-submit check (mirroring the existing `missingPicks` check for finished lines) that blocks with a specific error message rather than letting an empty-pool raw row silently vanish from submission if another row on the same form happened to be valid.

**What needed zero changes, confirmed before touching anything.** `get_client_report`, `get_serial_passport`, `stock_on_hand_rows`, `wip_rows`, and `useMoykaSerials.ts` all read `raw_dispatch_lines` directly by `serial` — none ever touched `chiqim_lines.raw_serial` or branched on `calibre_id`'s null-ness. Requirement 9 (passport shows partial raw collection) was already satisfied by existing code (`get_serial_passport`'s `rawDispatches` CTE joins through `chiqim_lines` but filters by the *draw's own* serial) — confirmed by hand-verification below, not just by inspection.

**The 8-file `calibre_id`/`raw_serial` ripple, now keyed on `line_kind`:**
- **`ChiqimForm.tsx` (major).** `rawSerial: string` → multi-select `Set<string>`; qty no longer required or auto-filled for raw rows (relabeled "Taxminiy miqdor (ixtiyoriy)"); submit inserts `line_kind`/nullable `qty_kg`, then batch-inserts the pool into `chiqim_line_raw_serials` — symmetric with the existing `chiqim_line_pallets` insert for finished rows, no 23505 race handling needed (line_id is freshly minted per submission). New local oldest-first sort for the picker (requirement 5, "aged raw moves before fresh") — `useMoykaSerials`'s own newest-first default is untouched, it stays load-bearing for §5.2 Window 1 elsewhere.
- **`OmborChiqimTab.tsx` (major).** State reshaped from one draft per line to an array of committed draws per line plus a per-line in-progress composer. Ombor picks a pooled serial (or types any other real serial — out-of-pool, warned, blocked from being *added* to the draft without a note, never blocking the overall finish) then weight+box mass, and adds it; a line can hold several draws, even against the same serial twice (requirement 6, confirmed the DB already allowed N `raw_dispatch_lines` rows per `chiqim_line_id` — no unique constraint ever existed there). `handleFinish` now inserts sequentially, not batched — mirrors `ChiqimForm.tsx`'s own established discipline against trusting batch-insert RETURNING order — because each draw's own returned id is what ties an out-of-pool note to the right row via the existing generic `notes` table (`entity_type='raw_dispatch_lines'`), reused as instructed rather than building a new note feature. A raw line's now-optional `qty_kg` also meant `lineStatus`/progress-bar/shortfall/aggregate-tone logic needed a "no target" path throughout this file and `chiqimScan.ts`'s `shortfallLines` — an un-estimated raw line shows its running total with no bar or shortfall claim, since there's nothing to fall short of.
- **`useOmborChiqimRequests.ts` (moderate).** `ChiqimLine` gained `line_kind` (kept snake_case, unlike this interface's other derived fields, to stay structurally compatible with `chiqimScan.ts`'s `ChiqimLineLike` which `request.lines` is passed into as-is) and `rawSerialPool: string[]` via a new `chiqim_line_raw_serials(serial)` join.
- **`FinishedChiqimList.tsx` (minor, extended per explicit confirmation).** `lineLabel` switches to `line_kind`; also fetches and shows the full pool (`Xom · A, B`) rather than degrading to a bare "Xom" — this is a receipt/history view, and a finished line's `reservedPallets` are already shown here in full, so showing less detail for raw would have been a regression in visibility, not a neutral simplification.
- **`useFinishedChiqimRequests.ts`, `useChiqimTrips.ts`, `chiqimScan.ts`, `chiqimScan.test.ts` (minor/mechanical).** Field swap throughout; `resolveScan`'s actual matching logic needed no change — a raw line's `calibre_id` is still always null either way, so it's still naturally never a scan candidate, exactly as that function's own pre-existing comment already explained.
- **One file beyond the original 8, found only by `tsc`, not by the earlier grep for `raw_serial`/`rawSerial` (which correctly wouldn't have caught it): `QorovulChiqimTab.tsx`** sums `qty_kg` across lines for its own requested-total display — broke once the type went nullable, fixed with the same `?? 0` treatment used everywhere else a raw line's absent target needs to contribute zero, not crash.

**Hand-verification against live seeded data.** Built under Nukus Agro Eksport (this log's own established client-report-arithmetic fixture), non-`TEST-`-prefixed plates. One raw line, no qty estimate, pooled with serial A (5,000 kg) and B (7,000 kg); a third serial C (3,000 kg) deliberately left OUT of the pool.
- Ombor drew 2,000 kg (net 1,980) from A and 1,500 kg (net 1,485) from B — both in-pool, matching the task's own worked example shape. `stock_on_hand_rows` confirmed: A → 3,020 kg (5,000−1,980 ✓), B → 5,515 kg (7,000−1,485 ✓).
- Drew 800 kg (net 790) from C, out-of-pool, with a required note — inserted successfully (never blocked), `notes` row written against the draw's own id. `stock_on_hand_rows` for C: 2,210 kg (3,000−790 ✓) — an out-of-pool draw subtracts from that serial's balance exactly like an in-pool one, since `useMoykaSerials` was never scoped by pool membership to begin with.
- `get_serial_passport` for both A and C show their own draw with the correct net/weight/box-mass/date, confirming requirement 9 without any code change needed for it.
- `get_client_report`'s `raw.reconciliation`: `totalReceivedKg=15000`, `xomKg=10745` (3,020+5,515+2,210), `cumulativeRawDispatchedKg=4255` (1,980+1,485+790), `balancesKg=0` — the three-bucket arithmetic from the prior task reconciles exactly against this new pool-shaped data, zero regression.
- Fixture deleted afterward via direct SQL (same no-void-mechanism precedent as prior fixtures) — all touched tables re-checked at 0 rows remaining.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b --noEmit` clean (after fixing the 4 nullable-`qty_kg` fallout errors above, including the `QorovulChiqimTab.tsx` one `tsc` caught beyond the original ripple list). Unit suite: 82/82. Full Playwright suite run once: 4/5 passed — the same `full-chain.spec.ts` failure as the prior task's own run, at the identical step (Ombor's "Moykada — chiqishi kutilmoqda" awaiting-output row), confirmed unrelated: that code path is `OmborTayyorTab.tsx`'s own Moyka-output list, not anything this task touched.

**🚩 Flagged, not silently edited.** SPEC.md §3.1 and §8 still describe the old one-serial-per-line model as 🔒 LOCKED ("pinned to one specific raw serial," "mutually exclusive with calibre_id/raw_serial," "two raw rows" for two serials) — now stale, same situation as §3.2.7 was before the prior task's cleanup. Left untouched pending an explicit decision, per the established pattern this session.

**Out of scope, not touched:** the notes feature itself (reused the existing generic mechanism, per the task's own instruction), Rezka, KN origin/state, opening-stock seeding, Ombor UI polish.

## 2026-08-02 — Ombor card polish, retroactively logged + one gap closed

**Context.** A task asking for visual-hierarchy polish on the Qabul qilingan (`OmborIntakeTab.tsx`) and Yuborishga tayyor (`OmborMoykaTab.tsx`) cards turned out to already be implemented — `git log` showed `14ef6f2` ("Ombor card polish: Qabul qilingan + Yuborishga tayyor visual hierarchy," merged via PR #77, 2026-07-30) landed *before* this session's own raw-dispatch work, but never got a DECISIONS.md entry. Rather than re-doing the work blind, verified the live code against every stated requirement line by line before touching anything.

**Verified already correct, no change needed:**
- Qabul qilingan: `SerialChip variant="emphasized"` (bold, dark) for the serial; the Netto figure wrapped in `font-bold text-slate-900`. Both read as primary data, as asked.
- Qabul qilingan: the gate-variance line (`Darvoza neta reys bo'yicha... farq qiladi`) softened to `text-sm italic text-slate-900` — quiet ordinary text, not a `StatusNote`/warning tone, kept (not removed) — confirmed on real data (`290726-071`: `+15 kg (+0.2%) farq qiladi`).
- Yuborishga tayyor: the same variance line is absent from this card entirely — confirmed on all 6 real cards currently in that window, none render it.
- Yuborishga tayyor: `Stat valueSize="compact"` (text-xl, not the default text-3xl) on all three tiles — fits cleanly, confirmed via computed style (`font-size: 20px`).
- Yuborishga tayyor: `SerialChip variant="prominent"` — blue background (`bg-blue-100`/`text-blue-900`), bold, larger padding — confirmed via computed style.
- `SerialChip`/`Stat` both carry opt-in variant props (`default`/`emphasized`/`prominent`, `default`/`compact`) — every other call site (grep-confirmed: 27 other files) passes no variant and renders byte-identical to before this work. `StatusNote` itself was never touched.

**Gap found and fixed.** Requirement 3's expanded-detail treatment for Aniq + arrival date was only half-done: `IntakeDetailView.tsx` moved them to the top and enlarged them (`text-xl`/`text-base`, bold) exactly as asked, but never added the "distinct background treatment — a tinted panel" the task also explicitly asked for — they sat in a plain, unstyled flex row, no different in kind from the label/value rows below beyond font size. Fixed by replacing the hand-rolled markup with two `<Stat tone="info" valueSize="compact" />` components — `info` being this codebase's own pre-existing "informational highlight" token (`bg-blue-50`/`border-blue-200`, `tokens.ts`), not a new color, and `Stat` itself the exact "existing design-system... stat component" the original task named. Confirmed live on serial `290726-071` (`Netto 5,141 kg`, matching this task's own worked example): the Aniq panel's resolved class is `rounded-md border p-3 border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-900 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/30`, computed `background-color: oklch(0.97 0.014 254.604)` — a real tinted panel, not just bold text.

**Scope guard.** Only `IntakeDetailView.tsx` touched this session (`git status` confirmed before and after); `SerialChip.tsx`/`Stat.tsx`/`StatusNote.tsx`/`tokens.ts` untouched. Grepped the whole `src/` tree for every non-default variant/tone this pass uses (`SerialChip variant=`, `Stat ... valueSize=`, `Stat ... tone="info"`) — confirmed contained to the three files in scope (`OmborIntakeTab.tsx`, `OmborMoykaTab.tsx`, `IntakeDetailView.tsx`); `RahbarHome.tsx`'s own pre-existing, unrelated `tone="info"` usage is a normal use of an already-general-purpose prop, not a scope leak.
