Hand-verified live against real Ombor data (dev server, logged in as Ombor): Qabul qilingan's bolder serial/Netto, the softened variance line, and the new Aniq/Sana tinted panel all confirmed by text content and computed CSS; Yuborishga tayyor's missing variance line, fitted stat tiles, and prominent serial chip all confirmed the same way. Full Playwright suite run once: 3/5 passed. Two failures: `full-chain.spec.ts` at the identical step as the prior two task runs this session (Ombor's "Moykada — chiqishi kutilmoqda" row, unrelated to anything in `IntakeDetailView.tsx`); `lab-packing-hard-gate.spec.ts`, new this run — traced its full flow (seeds via direct `window.supabase` calls through intake, never renders `IntakeDetailView.tsx`; the actual failure is in `LaboratorChiqimTab.tsx`, a page this change never touches) and confirmed unrelated, consistent with this suite's own documented no-branch-isolation limitation (shared live test DB) rather than a regression.

**Out of scope, not touched:** Rezka, opening stock, the notes feature, any logic/data change — this was presentation-only on the two named card types, as instructed.

## 2026-08-02 — Opening stock, Stage 1 (seed)

**Context.** The factory has pre-existing inventory predating the app — old washed pallets, old Konditirskiy (a weight pool with no pallets), old raw — that needs to be seeded, made collectible, and (later) re-washable, without ever being counted as a delivery in arrival/throughput reporting. Full design in an inspect-and-plan pass first (no code), confirmed with four additions before building: confirm old raw already flows through the normal Moyka send window; a qoldig'i old-stock filter that doesn't leak into event reports; the consume-and-mint mechanism generalized to accept a weight draw (not just pallets) so Rezka can reuse it later without redesign; and Menejer/Rahbar visibility confirmed end-to-end. `supabase/migrations/0048_opening_stock_stage1_seed.sql`, `0049_opening_stock_yield_rows_exclusion.sql`.

**The three kinds, as modelled.** Old washed = real `finished_pallets` rows (`is_old_stock`/`weight_is_estimate` booleans, independent — a later re-washed old-stock pallet is old-derived but not an estimate), anchored to one minted `kirim_orders`/`kirim_lines` serial per product type (5 total) rather than per pallet — mirrors how one real serial already produces multiple calibres. Old Konditirskiy = a genuinely new shape, `old_kn_pools` (owner_id, type_id, opening_kg) — no serial, no `kirim_lines` involvement at all, since KN opening stock was never individually tracked; balance is derived (`old_kn_collections`, the draw-down table, is Stage 2 scope — nothing collects against it yet, so the pool's balance is simply `opening_kg` for now). Old raw = zero new tables — a normal seeded `kirim_lines`/`storage_intake` row, flowing through the *existing* raw-dispatch machinery unchanged.

**The `origin` discriminator — the load-bearing addition.** `kirim_orders.origin` (`'delivery' | 'opening_stock' | 'internal_reprocess'`, default `'delivery'`) is what lets opening stock be visible as stock without being counted as a delivery. `report_kirim_rows` itself stays unfiltered (the qoldig'i/client-report *balance* paths need to keep seeing it) and just gains the column; the filter (`origin = 'delivery'`, a positive allowlist so a future Stage 3 re-wash's own `'internal_reprocess'` orders are automatically covered too, no second exclusion value needed later) is applied only at genuine **arrival** consumers, traced by reading each one's live definition rather than assumed: `report_rows` (Hisobot), and — confirmed via `pg_get_functiondef` that each reads `report_kirim_rows` directly, not through `report_rows`, so fixing `report_rows` alone would have missed all three — `rahbar_monthly_trends`, `rahbar_client_ranking`, `rahbar_product_mix`. `get_client_report`'s `raw_received_total`/`raw_received_by_type` (a period arrival aggregate, same category) get the identical treatment; `raw_opening_total`/`raw_closing_total`/`moykada_total` and the reconciliation object are balance figures and are deliberately left untouched, so the client report's own opening stock stays fully visible in the client's raw balance. The **departure/dispatch side stays unfiltered everywhere** — a real truck leaving with old-stock-derived material is a real event and should count in `volume_out`/`dispatched_kg`.

**`rahbar_exceptions()`'s `ageing_stock` branch** also excludes `is_old_stock` (2026-08-02 feedback) — it would flag permanently, forever, with no remediation possible, and a persistent unactionable entry devalues the whole exceptions list. `lab_overdue`/`high_loss`/`high_rewash` untouched.

**`stock_on_hand_rows`** gains `is_old_stock`/`weight_is_estimate` on the existing pallet/raw buckets, plus a new `old_kn` bucket (reads `old_kn_pools.opening_kg` directly — gets a small follow-up edit in Stage 2 once `old_kn_collections` exists to subtract from it). No toggle UI yet — that and the shared "Eski zaxira 2025-2026" date-label helper are the immediate code follow-up now that this migration is confirmed, not a second SQL gate.

**🐛 Real bug found during hand-verification, not by inspection — `yield_rows` had no origin awareness at all.** The seed mints a `'final'` `wash_cycles` row per old-washed serial (necessary: without a passing lab verdict, `chiqimScan.ts`'s hard gate hides the pallets entirely, and the task's own requirement was "immediately collectible"). But `yield_rows` treats any `'final'`-status wash cycle as a completed processing run — with `raw_consumed_kg` always 0 for these (no `moyka_sends` ever happened, they were seeded directly as pallets), it produced a phantom "0 kg in, 52,210 kg out" entry that `rahbar_monthly_trends()` then surfaced as a real January 2025 row: `output_kg: 52210`, a populated `calibre_mix`, `yield_serial_count: 5` — exactly the kind of Rahbar-facing distortion this whole task exists to prevent, just via a call site (`yield_rows`, not `report_kirim_rows`) the original plan hadn't traced. Fixed with the same `origin` column, but a *narrower* filter than everywhere else: `origin != 'opening_stock'` specifically, **not** `= 'delivery'` — a future Stage 3 re-wash mints an `'internal_reprocess'`-origin serial with a *real* wash cycle (real weighed `moyka_sends`, real output) that must keep counting normally in yield/loss/re-wash trends, per the already-confirmed "re-washing old stock is real processing" design point; blanket-filtering on `origin = 'delivery'` would have wrongly excluded that too.

**Hand-verified live, all three areas the confirmation asked for, via direct SQL (before the UI pieces exist):**
- **Qoldig'i**: `stock_on_hand_rows where is_old_stock` returns exactly three groups — `available` (finished pallets): 79 rows, 52,210 kg; `old_kn`: 4 rows, 103,936 kg; `raw_not_washed`: 1 row, 2,880 kg — all three kinds present, correctly flagged, summing to the full seed.
- **Hisobot**: `report_totals('kirim', '2025-01-01', '2025-01-01', ...)` → `(0,0,0,0,0)` — the minted arrivals contribute nothing.
- **Rahbar**: `rahbar_monthly_trends()` has *no row at all* for January 2025 after the `yield_rows` fix (confirming both the arrival side and the output/yield side are clean); `rahbar_client_ranking`/`rahbar_product_mix` for the seed-day period both return empty; `rahbar_exceptions()` returns no rows.
- **Global Export's client report**: period `2025-01-01`–`2025-01-01` (the seed day itself) → `openingKg=0, receivedKg=0`. Isolated further by type: Qand's `byType` entry for a period ending `2024-12-31` (before the seed) → `opening/closing/received` all `0`; for `2025-01-02` (the day *after* seeding) → `opening=closing=2880, received=0` — the old-raw contribution enters the balance immediately and is never counted as a period arrival, exactly bracketing the required behavior.

**Verification.** Both migrations applied directly (Supabase MCP, no branch — this project's own documented limitation). Every hand-verification query above re-run after the `yield_rows` fix landed, all clean.

**Out of scope, not touched (Stage 1):** `pallet_status`+`'consumed'`, `chiqim_lines.line_kind` widening, `old_kn_collections`, `serial_mint_sources`, the mint RPC — Stage 3. The qoldig'i toggle UI and per-kind rendering — Stage 2. Rezka, KN origin/state, the notes feature, the dehydration-reconciliation UI, per the original task's own scope.

## 2026-08-02 — Opening stock, Stage 2 (collection)

**Context.** Stage 1 seeded old-washed pallets, old KN pools, and an old-raw serial, invisible to arrival/throughput reporting. This stage makes all three collectible: Menejer names the line, Ombor collects it, balances draw down correctly. `supabase/migrations/0050_opening_stock_stage2_collection.sql`, `0051_get_client_report_opening_stock_leak_fix.sql`, `0052_get_serial_passport_old_stock_badge.sql`.

**Schema.** `chiqim_lines_kind_shape` widened from `finished|raw` to five: `old_washed` mirrors `finished`'s shape (calibre + qty required — real pallets, same `chiqim_line_pallets` mechanism); `old_kn`/`old_raw` mirror `raw`'s shape (no calibre, qty optional). `old_kn_collections` (id, chiqim_line_id, pool_id, collected_kg, collected_at, created_by) is a new append-only table, structurally a twin of `raw_dispatch_lines` but anchored to `pool_id` instead of a serial — KN has no serial identity (Stage 1 design). Same RLS shape too: `read_all`, `ombor_writes` (insert-only, no update/delete — committed only at "Yuklashni yakunlash," never amended after, same precedent as `raw_dispatch_lines`). `stock_on_hand_rows`'s `old_kn_rows` CTE now subtracts real collections (`opening_kg − Σold_kn_collections`) instead of Stage 1's `opening_kg` stub.

**Old-washed and old-raw dispatch needed zero reporting-view changes.** Traced via `pg_get_viewdef` before writing anything: `report_chiqim_rows` reads `finished_pallets`/`dispatch_manifest` directly, `report_raw_dispatch_rows` reads `raw_dispatch_lines` joined through `chiqim_lines` — neither reads `line_kind` at all. Reusing the exact same tables for `old_washed`/`old_raw` means their dispatch events flow into Hisobot/the client report unfiltered, automatically, matching the established "departure/dispatch side stays unfiltered everywhere" principle for free. **Old-KN reporting visibility (a new `report_old_kn_rows` kind, `get_client_report` extension) was explicitly deferred to a later pass, per confirmation** — `old_kn_collections` exists and drains the pool correctly; the collection event itself isn't yet surfaced as a Hisobot/client-report line.

**🐛 Bug #1, found via explicit instruction to sweep proactively (not waiting for each to surface) — `wip_rows.raw_not_sent` flagged the old-raw serial with a fabricated "578 days waiting to be sent to Moyka."** Same root cause as Stage 1's `yield_rows` bug: `storage_intake.confirmed_at` was deliberately backdated to the 2025-01-01 seed anchor for `effective_qty` purposes (correct for balance math), and this WIP category reads that same column to compute "how long has this been sitting unsent" — a real, actionable question for current raw, but a nonsense number for old-raw's fabricated anchor. Fixed the same way, same narrower exclusion (`origin != 'opening_stock'`, not the `= 'delivery'` allowlist).

**🐛 Bug #2, found the same sweep, more severe — `get_client_report`'s `loss_totals`/`loss_output`/`loss_main` leaked exactly like Stage 1's `yield_rows` did, into a client-facing report this time.** The seed's synthetic `'final'` `wash_cycles` rows (old-washed, minted only to satisfy the hard gate) have `completed_date` backdated to 2025-01-01 with zero real `moyka_sends` but real `finished_pallets` weight. Any client-report query spanning 2025-01-01 picked up all 5 old-washed serials via `loss_totals`, producing `processedBreakdown.lossKg = -52210` (sent_kg=0 minus real calibreKg) — confirmed live *before* fixing it: `get_client_report(<Global Export>, '2025-01-01','2025-01-31')->'raw'->'processedBreakdown'` returned `{"lossKg":-52210,"calibreKg":52210,"konditirskiyKg":0}`, i.e. 52 tonnes materializing from nowhere in a real client's loss figure. `quality_record` had the same exposure via `arrival_date` (old-raw too, via its own `arrival_date`, even without a `wash_cycle_status='final'` to trigger the loss-side bug) — a period-spanning query would list these serials with empty/misleading lab fields under a fabricated date. Both fixed with the same `cl.origin != 'opening_stock'` exclusion; every other CTE in the function byte-identical to the live definition, confirmed via `pg_get_functiondef` before writing the replacement. Re-verified after the fix: same query now returns `{"lossKg":0,"calibreKg":0,"konditirskiyKg":0}` and `qualityRecord: []`.

**Code layer, in the order built (old_kn's blank-bucket-label fix prioritized first per explicit instruction — it was already live and visible, not just a future risk):**
- **`stockOnHand.ts`/`useStockOnHand.ts`.** `StockBucket` widened with `old_kn` (label "Eski KN (havza)") — **this was a live, already-shipped defect**: `old_kn` rows have been flowing into the qoldig'i frontend's `select('*')` fetch since Stage 1 shipped `stock_on_hand_rows`, with no matching label/badge/sort-index entry, rendering blank. `StockOnHandRow.serial`/`.anchorDate`/`.daysHeld` widened to allow `null` (already true at the DB level for `old_kn` rows since Stage 1; the TS types just hadn't caught up — another latent gap, same root cause). Added `isOldStock`/`weightIsEstimate`, both piped through from the view.
- **`src/lib/oldStock.ts` (new).** One shared `formatStockDate(isOldStock, date)` helper — `"Eski zaxira 2025-2026"` spelled out exactly once, reused by qoldig'i's table and the passport, per explicit instruction not to repeat the label per screen.
- **`StockOnHandTable.tsx`.** ID column falls back to `barcode2 ?? serial ?? '—'` (old_kn has neither); Netto gets a `~` prefix when `weightIsEstimate`; Sana uses `formatStockDate`; Pasport link hidden (not just disabled) when `serial` is null; **Necha kun shows `—` for old stock**, found during this task's own live verification — `daysHeld` is derived from the exact same fabricated anchor the date column already hides, so "578 kun" was exactly as misleading as the raw date, just missed in the original date-only framing of the requirement.
- **`stockOnHand.ts` filter.** `oldStockOnly` is an exclusive-mode toggle, not an additive filter (`filters.oldStockOnly !== r.isOldStock` excludes either way) — current stock and old stock never render in the same table at once, matching "read as a distinct category, not scattered among current stock" literally rather than as a badge-within-the-same-list.
- **`StockOnHandTab.tsx`.** "Joriy zaxira / Eski zaxira" pill toggle, built directly in this file rather than extending the shared `ReportFilterBar` (also used by Hisobot) — same "shared component, scope via the local caller" call as the earlier card-polish task's own precedent.
- **`useMoykaSerials.ts`.** `MoykaSerial.isOldStock`, sourced from `kirim_orders.origin` (one extra column on the existing orders query, no new fetch). Splits the raw-serial pool in two: the existing Xom tab now excludes old stock, the new Eski Zaxira raw sub-picker includes only old stock — one balance, two entry points, never both for the same serial. **This closed a real, live gap found while building it**: before this change, Global Export's regular Kalibrlangan/Xom tabs already showed all 79 old-washed pallets and the old-raw serial mixed into the normal pickers (nothing had ever excluded them), with none of Stage 2's collection-specific handling (print-at-collection, book-vs-weighed awareness) — confirmed via live browser verification that the exclusion now correctly empties both regular pickers for old-stock-only type/calibre combinations.
- **`ChiqimForm.tsx`.** `LineRow.kind` widened to five (`PALLET_KINDS`/`POOL_KINDS`/`OLD_STOCK_KINDS` constants replace scattered `=== 'finished'`/`=== 'raw'` checks throughout). Third top-level tab "Eski zaxira" reveals a second row of three sub-buttons (Yuvilgan/KN/Xom) — `old_washed` reuses the finished picker's exact JSX (scoped to `isOldStock` pallets), `old_raw` reuses the raw pool picker's exact JSX (scoped to `isOldStock` serials), `old_kn` is new — no pool of candidates to pick from (one pool per type per owner), just a live balance readout (`oldKnPoolBalance`, reading the same `stock_on_hand_rows` `old_kn` bucket qoldig'i itself reads). The empty-pool guard (Task B's own "at least one pooled serial" rule) extended to `old_raw`; the pallet-picked guard extended to `old_washed`.
- **`chiqimScan.ts`.** `ChiqimLineLike.line_kind` widened to five — `resolveScan`'s candidate matching needed zero logic changes, the existing comment's own reasoning ("a raw line's calibre_id is always null, never matches a real pallet") already generalizes correctly: `old_washed` behaves like `finished` (calibre set, scanned normally), `old_kn`/`old_raw` behave like `raw` (calibre null, never a scan candidate).
- **`OmborChiqimTab.tsx`.** `old_washed` folds into the existing prep-list (widened filter, amber `Card tone="pending"` wrapper + "ESKI ZAXIRA" badge reusing the design system's own amber tone rather than inventing a color) — plus print-per-row (`Barcode2Display`, unchanged, reused as instructed) and a new print-all orchestrator (`PrintAllButton.tsx`) since these pallets were never labelled at seed time. `old_raw` folds into the existing raw-draw composer (widened filter, same amber card treatment) — 100% shared logic with `raw`, since old-raw dispatch is mechanically identical, just sourced from an old-stock-only pool. `old_kn` gets a new section — a simple measured-kg composer (`oldKnDrawsByRequest`/`oldKnComposerByRequest`, mirroring the raw-draw state shape but simpler: no serial, no box mass) showing the live pool balance and committing `old_kn_collections` rows at "Yuklashni yakunlash," resolving `pool_id` from the same `stockRows` `old_kn` bucket the balance readout itself uses (never a second source of truth). New `useOldKnCollectionsByRequest.ts` hook (sibling to `useRawDispatchLinesByRequest.ts`) feeds old-KN's own W2 history record.
- **`PrintAllButton.tsx` (new).** Sequential, not concurrent — the P1 Bluetooth printer is a single in-flight request (same constraint `Barcode2Display.tsx`'s own `printLabel` call already documents). Resumes from the last successful print on retry rather than reprinting from zero; native-only (`isP1PrinterAvailable()`), renders nothing on the web build where there's no batch-print path to sensibly offer.
- **`SerialPassportModal.tsx`/`get_serial_passport` (found via proactive sweep, not originally scoped to this stage — but the *original* inspect-and-plan task named the passport alongside qoldig'i/client report for "reading as distinct from current stock," and qoldig'i/client-report got that treatment while the passport hadn't yet).** `order.isOldStock` added to the RPC's `order` object (one field, every other CTE byte-identical, confirmed via `pg_get_functiondef`); the modal badges the Buyurtma section "ESKI ZAXIRA" and swaps the Sana row to the shared `formatStockDate` helper.

**Live browser hand-verification (dev server, real seeded data, TEST-STAGE2 plate).** Menejer's ChiqimForm: all three Eski Zaxira sub-pickers confirmed against real seed values — old-washed Subxon/Kalibr 6 picker showed exactly 17 real pallets (16×720+1×20 = 11,540 kg, matching the seed precisely); old-KN Subxon showed "Havzada mavjud: 38,202 kg"; old-raw Qand showed serial `020826-039` at "2,880 kg mavjud." Confirmed both exclusions live: switching the same Subxon/Kalibr 6 selection to the regular Kalibrlangan tab returned "no matching pallet" (old-washed correctly hidden from current-stock picker); switching Qand to the regular Xom tab returned "no matching raw serial" (old-raw correctly hidden). Submitted a real 3-line TEST- request (one pallet from each of the three kinds) and confirmed Ombor's card renders all three sections correctly — amber "ESKI ZAXIRA" badges, the prep-list's print controls, the old-KN composer's live balance, the old-raw composer's pool serial — before voiding the request (`voided_at`, the `chiqim_requests_release_reservations` trigger confirmed releasing the pallet reservation) rather than committing any real collection, since old-KN/old-raw draws would permanently consume real seeded inventory with no undo path. qoldig'i: "Joriy zaxira"/"Eski zaxira" toggle confirmed exclusive (never both at once); Eski zaxira totals matched the seed exactly — Mavjud 52,210 kg, Xom yuvilmagan 2,880 kg, Eski KN (havza) 103,936 kg; weight-estimate `~` prefix and the `Necha kun`→`—` fix both confirmed visually after the fix landed. `get_serial_passport`'s `isOldStock` confirmed `true` for a real old-washed serial via direct RPC call.

**Verification.** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean throughout (re-run after every file group). `npm test`: 82/82 unchanged (`resolveScan`/`shortfallLines` widened types, zero behavior change — confirmed by the existing suite passing as-is). Playwright not re-run this pass (no `.spec.ts` file touches CHIQIM/qoldig'i/passport routes this task didn't already hand-verify live end to end; the suite's own established shared-DB flakiness, documented in every prior entry this session, would add noise without new signal for a UI/RPC change already verified against real data).

**🚩 Real process gap, caught by the PR's Netlify check, not by this session's own verification.** `npx tsc --noEmit` (what this session ran) is a silent no-op against this repo's root `tsconfig.json` — it's a solution-style config (`"files": []`, only `references` to `tsconfig.app.json`/`tsconfig.node.json`), so plain `--noEmit` from the root checks literally zero files and always exits clean regardless of real errors. The correct invocation, used in every earlier DECISIONS.md entry's own "Verification" line, is `npx tsc -b --noEmit` (build mode, which resolves the referenced projects). Netlify's `npm run build` (`tsc -b && vite build`) caught what the wrong local command couldn't: `OLD_STOCK_KINDS` declared-unused in `OmborChiqimTab.tsx` (dead code left over from an earlier draft), an unused `StatusNote` import and an invalid `size="sm"` (this codebase's `ButtonSize` is `'lg' | 'md'` only) in `PrintAllButton.tsx`. All three fixed; `npm run build` and `npx tsc -b --noEmit` both clean afterward, 82/82 unit tests unaffected. Flagging the command distinction here so a future session doesn't repeat it.

**Out of scope, not touched (Stage 2):** old-KN reporting visibility in Hisobot/`get_client_report` (deferred per confirmation, see above), `pallet_status`+`'consumed'`, `serial_mint_sources`, the consume-and-mint RPC, the old-stock-to-Moyka form — Stage 3. Rezka, the dehydration-reconciliation UI, the notes feature (beyond what Stage 3 will reuse), per the original task's own scope.

## 2026-08-02 — Phantom inbound deliveries in Qorovul's gate + Ombor's intake (Stage 1 fallout, shipped separately)

**Context.** Found while tracing where a Stage 3 minted order would land, not by a bug report. Neither `useKirimTrips` (Qorovul's KIRIM gate) nor `useIntakeLines` (Ombor's Skladga KIRIM) filtered `kirim_orders.origin`, so the 5 opening-stock anchor orders Stage 1 seeded appeared as real inbound work: Qorovul saw **5 phantom trucks queued to be weighed** (`status='kutilmoqda'` + no `gate_weighings` row is literally that screen's "not started" predicate) and Ombor saw **5 phantom deliveries queued to be accepted** (no `storage_intake` row is literally its "pending" predicate). Confirmed from data plus the filter predicates before touching anything.

**Why this was worse than the four earlier leaks.** Those distorted *reports*. These were *action* screens with a destructive action available: accepting one would have written a real `storage_intake` row and materialised **~52 tonnes of phantom raw stock** in qoldig'i. Stage 1 deliberately gave old-washed serials no `storage_intake` (correct for the balance maths) — which is exactly what makes them look like un-processed inbound deliveries to the two roles whose whole job is processing inbound deliveries.

**Fix.** `.eq('origin','delivery')` on both hooks — a positive allowlist, so Stage 3's `internal_reprocess` origin is covered by construction rather than needing a second exclusion later. Shipped as its own branch/PR ahead of Stage 3 at the user's explicit instruction, since it was visible to staff in production. Verified live: Qorovul reads "Kutilmoqda 0 / Faol reys yo'q", Ombor reads "Kutilayotgan reys yo'q", and every genuine delivery still shows in both.

## 2026-08-02 — Opening stock, Stage 3 (the mint mechanism and re-washing)

**Context.** The last of the three opening-stock stages, and the one Rezka inherits: re-washing old washed stock requires consuming finished pallets and minting a new serial, which is structurally identical to Rezka source 2. Designed generic from day one. `supabase/migrations/0053_pallet_status_consumed.sql`, `0054_serial_mint_sources.sql`, `0055_mint_serial_rpc.sql`, `0056_get_serial_passport_mint_origin.sql`.

**The mint mechanism, split in two on purpose.** `mint_serial_from_sources(...)` is generic and knows nothing about Moyka — validate sources, mark pallets `consumed`, mint `kirim_orders`/`kirim_lines` with `origin='internal_reprocess'`, write `serial_mint_sources`, return the serial. Rezka calls it unchanged and sends its serial to cutting instead. `send_old_stock_to_moyka(...)` is a thin old-stock wrapper that calls it and then does the `wash_cycles` upsert, the `moyka_sends` insert and the origin note — **all in one transaction**, so a failure can never strand consumed pallets with no send behind them. Both are `security definer` because `kirim_orders`/`kirim_lines` are menejer-insert-only under RLS and Ombor is the actor; both therefore carry an explicit `my_role()` gate, since definer bypasses RLS entirely.

**Both source shapes built; only the pallet path wired.** `serial_mint_sources` carries an explicit `source_kind` discriminator with a check constraint enforcing exactly one populated shape. `old_kn_pools`' balance was generalized *today* to `opening_kg − Σold_kn_collections − Σserial_mint_sources(weight_kg)` in both `stock_on_hand_rows` and the RPC's own guard — the third term is always zero until Rezka calls it, but getting it right now is the difference between Rezka working and Rezka silently under-counting a pool. A partial unique index (`source_barcode2 where source_kind='pallet'`) is the real guarantee a pallet can be consumed once and only once; the RPC's `for update` pre-check just turns that race into a readable error instead of a 23505.

**🚩 Deviation from the sketched spec, taken deliberately: `source_pool_id`, not `source_pool_type_id`.** Pools are unique per *(owner, type)*, so matching a weight draw on type alone would over-subtract from every owner holding that type the moment a second client has KN. Pointing at `old_kn_pools(id)` is unambiguous and mirrors `old_kn_collections.pool_id`. Flagged before building and approved.

**`declared_qty` = the WEIGHED figure, not the book figure.** This is the load-bearing modelling choice. It keeps `yield_rows`' `raw_received == raw_consumed`, so wash loss is pure processing loss and no false `capped_serials` alarm fires when the scale happens to read over book. Storage loss stays derivable and never stored: `Σ(book weights via serial_mint_sources → finished_pallets) − moyka_sends.qty_kg`. Verified live at 60 kg on the test mint.

**Re-wash output gets `is_old_stock = false`** — contradicting a line written in Stage 1, deliberately. `true` would be wrong twice: the qoldig'i toggle is exclusive, so freshly produced sellable stock would vanish from *Joriy zaxira*, and its date would read "Eski zaxira 2025-2026" when it was produced today. Lineage lives in `serial_mint_sources`, the notes and the passport instead. Confirmed live: the new pallet reads `is_old_stock=false / bucket=available`.

**🔑 The rule that resolved a real contradiction in this stage's own design: `finished_pallets.status` is a CURRENT-LOCATION field, not a did-this-happen field.** It was initially flagged as a bug that `useMoykaOutput` keeps counting consumed pallets in a serial's `received`, while simultaneously arguing `yield_rows` should keep counting them so a finalized serial's loss never moves retroactively. Those conflict; the user caught it. Resolution — both keep counting, and the split is by *question asked*:

- "Where is this pallet **now**?" → must filter status: `stock_on_hand_rows`, `useAvailableFinishedStock`, `chiqimScan.resolveScan`, `get_client_report.client_pallets`, and `report_chiqim_rows` — which is precisely why that last one **was** a real bug and is fixed here: a consumed pallet fell through its `CASE` to `'omborda'`, a location claim that is false. New `'ishlatilgan'` branch added, plus the matching `PalletStatusFilter` value and Hisobot filter option.
- "What did this serial **produce**?" → must ignore status: `yield_rows.output` and `useMoykaOutput.received`. The pallet was genuinely produced; consumption is a later event in a *different* serial's life, and subtracting it rewrites history. Concretely, excluding it would also break on-screen arithmetic, since a finalized serial's `lossPct` is the **locked** `wash_cycles.final_loss_pct` — "sent 3,500 · received 2,900 · loss 17.1%" would silently become "received 2,400 · loss 17.1%".

`bekor_qilindi` is the exception that proves the rule: `useMoykaOutput` skips voided pallets because voiding *retracts the production event itself* ("this was mis-recorded"). Consuming asserts the opposite. Different meanings, different treatment.

**The timestamp-leak audit — every consumer traced, verified live on a real end-to-end mint.** Four earlier leaks (`yield_rows`, `rahbar_monthly_trends`, `wip_rows`, `get_client_report`) all came from Stage 1's *backdated* seed timestamps. A minted serial's timestamps are **real** (today), which is exactly why the correct treatment inverts: `opening_stock` must not count as processing, `internal_reprocess` must. Test mint `020826-040`: 2 Qand K6 pallets, book 1,040 kg, weighed **980**, output 890.

| Consumer | Expected | Observed |
|---|---|---|
| `report_rows` / Hisobot `report_totals` | excluded (not an arrival) | 0 rows, `kg_in` 0 |
| `rahbar_monthly_trends.volume_in_kg` | excluded | 0 |
| `rahbar_client_ranking` / `rahbar_product_mix` | excluded | 0 / 0 |
| `get_client_report.receivedKg` | excluded | 0 |
| `report_kirim_rows` | **unfiltered** (balance consumers need it) | 1 row |
| `yield_rows` | **included** — real processing | received 980 = consumed 980, **overage 0**, output 890, loss **9.2%** |
| `rahbar_monthly_trends` output / raw_consumed | **included** | 890 / 980 |
| `get_client_report.processedBreakdown` | **included** | loss 90 kg · 9.2%, processed 980 |
| `get_client_report.qualityRecord` | **included** — a real lab test happened | 1 row |
| `stock_on_hand_rows` (minted serial) | no phantom raw | 0 rows |
| `wip_rows` / `rahbar_exceptions()` | silent | 0 / 0 |
| `report_chiqim_rows` (the 2 consumed pallets) | `'ishlatilgan'`, never `'omborda'` | `ishlatilgan` |

Loss reads **9.2%** from the weighed 980, not the 14.4% the book 1,040 would have produced — the test figures were chosen so the two are distinguishable. qoldig'i old-washed fell 52,210 → 51,170 kg (−1,040 book) and 79 → 77 pallets. `wip_rows` stays silent because `raw_not_sent` needs a `storage_intake` row (the mint deliberately creates none) and `provisional_weight` needs `provisional` (false without intake); `moyka_not_returned`/`awaiting_lab` would fire on a genuinely delayed re-wash, which is correct and wanted.

**Notes wiring.** Neither `LaboratorChiqimTab` nor the passport rendered notes at all before. Both now read the same generic `entity_type='moyka'` key the mint writes to, so there is one note, not three mechanisms. Verified live: the lab's Window-1 card shows *"Eski zaxiradan qayta yuvish: 2 ta eski pallet ishlatildi, kitob bo'yicha ~1040 kg, tarozida 980 kg yuborildi."* The finished-output lineage note is written at receipt in `OmborTayyorTab` against the serial (naming the barcode2 in the body) rather than a new per-pallet `entity_type` — deliberately, so it is readable in three existing surfaces immediately instead of being an orphan nobody displays. The passport renders notes read-only (it is a record, not a workspace); adding stays where the work happens.

**Passport.** New "Kelib chiqishi — qayta ishlangan" section, rendered FIRST for a minted serial because it *is* that serial's beginning and the Buyurtma/Darvoza sections below are near-empty for it by design. Shows source pallet count, book total, weighed sent, the explicit storage-difference line, and a per-pallet table (barcode2 · calibre · source serial · book kg). Omitted entirely for ordinary delivered serials, matching the passport's existing "missing shows nothing" rule.

**🐛 KNOWN DEFECT, logged not fixed — consuming a pallet retroactively alters closed-period client reports.** `get_client_report.client_pallets` filters `fp.status = 'in_stock'` with no as-of-date, so a pallet consumed (or voided) today silently drops out of *last month's* finished opening/closing figures too. This is pre-existing behaviour that voided pallets already exhibit, but Stage 3 makes it reachable routinely, and it lands on a **client-facing document**. A correct fix needs `client_pallets` to reconstruct status as-of `p_to` from event dates rather than reading the current flag. Out of scope here; recorded so it does not get lost.

**🚩 One real regression, caught by the suite and fixed in source.** The old-stock button was first rendered in its own wrapper div between the "1 · Yuborishga tayyor" heading and the serial list. `full-chain.spec.ts` locates that window's rows via `heading → following-sibling::div[1]`, so the new div silently displaced the list to `div[2]` and the spec stopped finding any row. Fixed by moving the button *inside* the existing list container (also better: one consistent `space-y-2` rhythm), not by editing the test. **Verified it was genuinely mine and genuinely fixed** by re-running: the failure moved from line 262 to line 318 — and line 318 then failed *identically on a clean `main` checkout*, proving that second failure is the long-standing pre-existing one this log has recorded at the same step across three prior task runs, not Stage 3 fallout.

**Verification.** `npm run build` (`tsc -b && vite build`) clean — using the build-mode invocation, per the process gap recorded in the Stage 2 entry. Unit suite 82/82. Playwright: 4/5, the one failure proven pre-existing on `main` as above. Full end-to-end hand-verification on real data through four roles (Ombor mint+send → Laborator verdict → Ombor pack → Tugallash), plus the report table above.

**Out of scope, not touched:** Rezka itself, the dehydration-reconciliation UI, KN origin/state tracking, old-KN dispatch reporting (deferred in Stage 2), and the closed-period client-report defect logged above.
